### [107] [Superconducting and charge-ordered phases from Dirac quantum spin liquids on the triangular lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05277)
*Andreas Feuerpfeil,Ronny Thomale,Subir Sachdev,Pietro M. Bonetti*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的理论框架，描述三角晶格量子自旋液体在压力或掺杂下转变为超导和电荷有序态的机制。该框架基于U(1)狄拉克自旋液体及其有能隙的Z₂和手性衍生态，通过自旋子（chargon）的Higgs凝聚来实现从分数量子化态到常规超导/电荷有序态的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角晶格量子自旋液体绝缘体在压力或掺杂下可转变为超导态，且在红外光驱动下表现出太赫兹电导增强现象。需要建立通用的理论框架来理解这些从量子自旋液体到超导/电荷有序相的转变机制。

Method: 基于U(1)狄拉克自旋液体中的费米子自旋子框架，引入自旋（无自旋）带电e的玻色子chargon（包括doublon和holon）的Higgs凝聚机制。利用狄拉克自旋子的投影对称群唯一确定chargon的对称性和色散关系，构建chargon能带极小值附近的低能有效理论，并通过规范不变的chargon Higgs场的复合算符刻画各相的有序参数。

Result: 得到的相图包含丰富多样的有序态，包括d+id超导、电荷密度波、键密度波和配对密度波等。该框架统一解释了量子自旋液体绝缘体向超导/电荷有序相的转变，以及相关的太赫兹电导增强等实验现象。

Conclusion: 狄拉克自旋子的投影对称群决定了chargon的对称性和色散，从而决定了转变后有序相的类型和性质。该理论为理解三角晶格量子自旋液体的压力/掺杂诱导超导转变提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: Triangular-lattice quantum spin-liquid insulators are observed to undergo transitions to superconductivity under pressure or doping, and exhibit enhanced terahertz conductivity when driven by mid-infrared light. We present a general theoretical framework for the emergence of superconducting and charge-ordered phases from a U(1) Dirac spin liquid with fermionic spinons, as well as from its gapped $\mathbb{Z}_2$ and chiral descendants. Numerical studies have provided substantial evidence for these spin-liquid states.
  The spin-liquid phase hosts fractionalized Dirac spinons coupled to an emergent gauge field, whereas the superconducting and charge-ordered phases are conventional, with neither fractionalized excitations nor emergent gauge dynamics. The transition between these phases is driven by the Higgs condensation of spinless charge-$e$ bosonic chargons (``doublons'' and ``holons''). We show that the projective symmetry group of the Dirac spinons uniquely determines the symmetry and dispersion of the chargons, allowing us to construct an effective low-energy theory near the chargon band minima. Gauge-invariant composites of the chargon Higgs fields provide the order parameters characterizing the phases. The resulting phase diagram contains a rich variety of ordered states, including $d+id$ superconductivity, charge-density waves, bond-density waves, and pair-density waves.

</details>


### [108] [Self-dual $S_3$ gauge theory in 2+1d: lattice model and topological phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05294)
*Da-Chuan Lu,Chong Wang,Ashvin Vishwanath*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文首次构造了非阿贝尔推广的电磁自对偶格点模型：在$S_3$量子双$\mathcal{D}(S_3)$的张量积希尔伯特空间上，通过晶格平移实现交换非阿贝尔电荷子$C$与磁通子$F$的$\mathbb{Z}_2^{\mathrm{em}}$任意子置换对称性。该模型的锯齿形边界无需微调即可实现由四临界Ising CFT描述的无隙临界边缘态。体态支持三个独立的保$\mathbb{Z}_2^{\mathrm{em}}$玻色子微扰，分别驱动$\mathcal{D}(S_3)$进入不同的有隙相，三种独立方法（范畴论任意子凝聚、微观晶格哈密顿量、Chern-Simons-Higgs理论）得到一致结果。该构造可推广至无限族自对偶二面体量子双$\mathcal{D}(D_{2n})$，且模型无符号问题，可进行大规模数值模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁自对偶性是格点规范理论中具有精确格点实现的重要对偶对称性范例，此前仅有$\mathbb{Z}_2$规范理论的实现。作者的目标是将这一构造推广到非阿贝尔情形，探索非阿贝尔任意子置换对称性及其在边界和相变中的物理后果。

Method: 作者在$\mathcal{D}(S_3)$量子双模型上，通过半格晶格平移构造电磁自对偶对称性，实现交换非阿贝尔电荷子与磁通子的$\mathbb{Z}_2^{\mathrm{em}}$对称性。分析锯齿形边界得到四临界Ising CFT描述的无隙边缘态。体态相变分析采用三种独立方法：范畴理论任意子凝聚、微观晶格哈密顿量、Chern-Simons-Higgs理论，三者交叉验证。构造亦推广至一般二面体群$D_{2n}$。

Result: 三个独立体态微扰驱动系统进入三种不同的有隙相，三种分析方法给出统一结果。提出的最小凝聚原理指出：增殖某个玻色子任意子通常驱动包含它的最小可凝聚代数发生凝聚，对称性关联的凝聚态作为简并真空自发破缺任意子置换对称性。模型无符号问题，适用于大规模数值模拟。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了非阿贝尔量子双模型电磁自对偶性的首次精确格点实现，建立了非阿贝尔Chern-Simons-Higgs理论相变的统一图景，并提出最小凝聚原理作为一般性指导原则。无限族推广与无符号问题使其成为数值研究非阿贝尔任意子凝聚和相变的理想平台。

Abstract: Electric-magnetic self-duality of the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory, realized microscopically as a half-lattice-translation exchanging electric charge and magnetic flux, has been an influential example of a duality symmetry with an exact lattice realization. We construct the first non-Abelian generalization of this construction: a lattice model of the $S_3$ quantum double $\mathcal{D}(S_3)$ on a tensor product Hilbert space in which the $\mathbb{Z}^{\mathrm{em}}_2$ anyon-permutation symmetry, exchanging the non-Abelian chargeon $C$ and fluxon $F$, is realized via lattice translation. Consequently we find that the zigzag boundary termination of the model realizes, without fine-tuning, a gapless critical edge state described by the tetracritical Ising CFT. The bulk admits three independent $\mathbb{Z}_2^{\mathrm{em}}$-preserving bosonic perturbations, driving $\mathcal{D}(S_3)$ into distinct gapped phases. We analyze these transitions by three independent methods: category-theoretic anyon condensation, microscopic lattice Hamiltonians, and Chern-Simons-Higgs theory, which all agree, yielding a unified picture. These examples motivate a minimal-condensation principle: proliferating a bosonic anyon generically drives condensation of a minimal condensable algebra containing it, with symmetry-related condensates appearing as degenerate vacua that spontaneously break the anyon-permutation symmetry. Our model construction extends to an infinite family of self-dual dihedral quantum doubles $\mathcal{D}(D_{2n})$. Notably, each model is sign-problem-free, opening the door to large-scale numerical exploration of the phases of non-Abelian Chern-Simons-Higgs theories.

</details>


### [109] [Strongly Enhanced Charge-Density Waves and Correlated Insulating State in Atomically Thin 1$T$-TaS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05532)
*Gan Liu,Yulu Liu,Qiling Luo,Zhentao Huang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Meiyu Wang,Jinsheng Wen,Yi Lu,Xiaoxiang Xi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过变温拉曼光谱和电输运测量，系统研究了1T-TaS₂中厚度依赖的电荷密度波（CDW）相变。研究发现各CDW相可持续到单层极限，随厚度减薄相变温度升高、片电阻增大、载流子局域长度缩短，且单层中一阶滞后的CCDW-NCCDW相变消失。计算表明，减薄导致的出平面屏蔽减弱增强库仑相互作用（尤其非局域成分）是CDW增强的根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解原子级薄层极限下电子关联、电子-声子耦合和层间耦合在CDW基态及相变动力学中的协同作用，为二维CDW系统中关联相的工程化调控开辟途径。

Method: 采用温度依赖的拉曼光谱和电输运测量，并结合理论计算分析不同厚度1T-TaS₂的CDW相变行为。

Result: （1）非公度、近公度、公度三种CDW相在单层极限仍存在；（2）厚度减薄导致相变温度升高、片电阻增加数个量级、载流子局域长度急剧缩短；（3）单层中一阶滞后的CCDW-NCCDW相变缺失；（4）计算揭示减薄后出平面屏蔽减弱增强库仑相互作用（尤其非局域部分）是CDW增强的机制。

Conclusion: 电子关联、电子-声子相互作用和层间耦合在原子薄1T-TaS₂的基态与相变动力学中共同发挥关键作用，为二维CDW系统中关联相的工程化提供了新路径。

Abstract: We investigate thickness-dependent charge-density-wave (CDW) transitions in 1$T$-TaS$_2$ using temperature-dependent Raman spectroscopy and electrical transport. Raman measurements show that the incommensurate, nearly commensurate, and commensurate CDW phases persist down to the monolayer limit. As the thickness is reduced, the transition temperatures increase, accompanied by an orders-of-magnitude rise in sheet resistance and a sharp reduction in the carrier localization length. The first-order hysteretic CCDW-NCCDW transition is uniquely absent in the monolayer. Calculations suggest that the enhanced CDW in thin layers originates from strengthened Coulomb interactions due to reduced out-of-plane screening, particularly in the nonlocal component. These findings highlight the cooperative roles of electron correlation, electron-phonon interaction, and interlayer coupling in shaping the ground state and transition dynamics of atomically thin 1$T$-TaS$_2$, opening pathways for engineering correlated phases in two-dimensional CDW systems.

</details>


### [110] [Local Spin Excitations Mediate Quasiparticle Breakdown in the Orbital-Selective Mott Phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05556)
*Yuekun Niu,Yu Ni,Jia-Ming Wang,Zhong-Yi Lu,Yun Song,Shiping Feng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过动力学平均场理论研究双带Hubbard模型，发现Hund耦合的自旋翻转和Ising分量会产生局域自旋激发（LSEs），这些激发在不同轨道间耦合电子，导致轨道选择性Mott相（OSMP）中准粒子破裂——这是该现象的基本微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道选择性Mott相（OSMP）传统上被描述为有效解耦轨道中局域电子与巡游电子的共存，但近期证据表明存在准粒子破裂现象，这超出了现有图景的范畴，而其微观起源尚不清楚。

Method: 采用动力学平均场理论（DMFT）对双带Hubbard模型进行研究，系统分析Hund耦合中自旋翻转项和Ising型项的贡献，并通过对这两个分量进行移除的对照实验来验证其作用。

Result: 研究表明Hund耦合的自旋翻转和Ising型分量产生局域自旋激发（LSEs），这些激发在不同轨道之间耦合电子，重新归一化准粒子寿命和结合能，从而破坏OSMP中良好定义的准粒子。移除这两个分量后，相干准粒子行为得以恢复，且两个带的电荷动力学完全解耦。

Conclusion: 电子与局域自旋激发（LSEs）的耦合是导致OSMP内准粒子破裂的根本机制，这一发现超越了传统"局域与巡游电子共存"的简单图景。

Abstract: The orbital-selective Mott phase (OSMP) is commonly described as a coexistence of localized and itinerant electrons within effectively decoupled orbitals, but emerging evidence for quasiparticle breakdown points to physics beyond this picture, whose microscopic origin remains unknown. Using dynamical mean-field theory for the two-band Hubbard model, we show that the spin-flip and Ising-type components of Hund's coupling generate local spin excitations (LSEs). These LSEs couple electrons between different orbitals, renormalize quasiparticle lifetimes and binding energies, and thereby destroy well-defined quasiparticles in the OSMP. Removing these two components of Hund's coupling restores coherent quasiparticle behavior and fully decouples the charge dynamics of the two bands. Our results therefore identify electronic coupling to LSEs as the fundamental mechanism driving quasiparticle breakdown within the OSMP.

</details>


### [111] [Helical-to-Fan Transitions under Magnetic Fields in the Noncentrosymmetric Tetragonal Magnet EuRhGe$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05709)
*Takeshi Matsumura,Hiroto Nitta,Hironori Nakao,Masashi Kakihana,Masato Hedo,Takao Nakama,Yoshichika Ōnuki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过共振X射线衍射研究了EuRhGe₃（非中心对称体心四方磁体，空间群I4mm）的磁结构。在T_N=12 K以下，该材料表现出非公度传播矢量q=(0,0,0.809)的螺旋磁有序，磁矩位于ab平面内，相邻层间以145.8°恒定的旋转角转动。磁场沿a轴施加时，2q谐波峰的出现表明圆形螺旋逐渐畸变为螺旋磁孤子格子态，并在3.8 T时锁定为q=0.8的公度结构。在5 T以上的相边界之后，系统失去螺旋性，进入自旋翻转的xyz扇（椭圆锥）态，最终在高场下进入无c轴分量的平面xy扇相。该材料提供了从圆形螺旋到自旋翻转xyz扇（椭圆锥），再到平面xy扇结构的完整场致结构序列的原型范例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于验证理论预测的螺旋磁体中完整的场致结构演化序列。理论预测螺旋磁体在磁场作用下应依次经历圆形螺旋→螺旋磁孤子格子→自旋翻转xyz扇（椭圆锥）→平面xy扇的转变，但此前缺乏实验体系的系统验证。EuRhGe₃作为非中心对称体心四方磁体，为测试这一理论预测提供了理想的实验平台。

Method: 采用共振X射线衍射技术（resonant X-ray diffraction）对EuRhGe₃单晶的磁结构进行系统研究。在零场下通过磁散射峰确定传播矢量和螺旋结构；在磁场施加于a轴方向时，通过监测2q二次谐波峰的出现和发展来追踪螺旋畸变过程，并利用衍射峰的位置和强度变化确定各场致相的磁性结构。

Result: 实验揭示了EuRhGe₃完整的场致磁结构演化过程：(1) 零场下为q=(0,0,0.809)的非公度圆形螺旋，磁矩在ab平面内以145.8°恒定转角旋转；(2) 施加a轴磁场后，2q峰出现，圆形螺旋畸变为螺旋磁孤子格子态；(3) 3.8 T时锁定为q=0.8的公度结构；(4) 5 T以上进入自旋翻转xyz扇（椭圆锥）态，磁矩主要沿b轴振荡并伴有小的c轴分量；(5) 更高场下进入平面xy扇相，c轴分量消失。

Conclusion: EuRhGe₃是首个实验上完整实现理论预测的全序列场致磁结构的螺旋磁体——从圆形螺旋到自旋翻转xyz扇（椭圆锥），最终到平面xy扇。该材料为验证和深化螺旋磁体中场致磁性结构理论提供了关键实验证据和原型范例。

Abstract: The magnetic structure of EuRhGe$_3$, a noncentrosymmetric body-centered tetragonal magnet with the space group $I4mm$, has been investigated by resonant X-ray diffraction. Below $T_{\text{N}}=12$ K, EuRhGe$_3$ undergoes a helical magnetic ordering with an incommensurate propagation vector $q=(0, 0, 0.809)$, in which the magnetic moments lie in the $ab$ plane and rotate by a constant turn angle of $145.8^{\circ}$ between adjacent layers. When a magnetic field is applied along the $a$ axis at 2 K, a second-harmonic $2q$ peak develops, indicating that the circular helix is gradually distorted into a helimagnetic soliton-lattice state, which eventually undergoes a lock-in transition to the commensurate structure with $q=0.8$ at 3.8 T. Above the subsequent phase boundary at 5 T, the helicity is lost, and a spin-flop $xyz$-fan (elliptic conical) state is realized, in which the moments oscillate predominantly along the $b$ axis but are accompanied by a small $c$-axis component. At higher fields, the system enters a conventional planar $xy$-fan phase without a $c$-axis component. EuRhGe$_3$ provides a prototypical example of a helimagnet that exhibits a full sequence of field-induced structures, evolving from a circular helix to a spin-flop $xyz$-fan (elliptic conical), and finally to a planar $xy$-fan structure, which has been theoretically predicted.

</details>


### [112] [Correlated topological-polarization surface states in the narrow-gap insulator FeSb2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05887)
*Takahiro Iwagaki,Hideki Matsuoka,Ginta Hoshino,Kanata Watanabe,Shungo Aoyagi,Shunsuke Kitou,Yuiga Nakamura,Motoaki Hirayama,Takashi Koretsune,Naoya Kanazawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明外延FeSb2薄膜中存在由拓扑极化产生的金属极性表面态，其受体相强关联电子调控，并展示了非互易表面输运仅在体相Fe 3d轨道占据发生关联驱动重构后才出现，且可通过静电门控诱导表面进入铁磁或可能交变磁态，确立了拓扑极化作为关联拓扑相设计原理的地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强电子关联和能带拓扑各自能产生丰富的量子相，但二者的元素需求相互冲突，难以在同一材料中结合。本文旨在通过拓扑极化这一途径，将能带拓扑扩展到强关联的3d过渡金属化合物中，从而弥合这两大领域的鸿沟。

Method: 采用外延薄膜生长制备窄带隙绝缘体FeSb2薄膜，通过输运测量表征表面态的非互易输运行为，结合静电门控调控表面电子态，并利用体相Fe 3d轨道占据的关联驱动重构来验证体-边对应关系。

Result: FeSb2外延薄膜展现出拓扑极化起源的金属极性表面态，该表面态由体相强关联控制；非互易表面输运仅在体相关联驱动重构的起始温度以下出现，提供了关联拓扑系统中体-边对应的直接证据；静电门控可将该关联表面驱动跨越量子相变进入铁磁或可能的交变磁态。

Conclusion: 拓扑极化可作为设计关联拓扑相的一般性原理，适用于广泛材料体系，为在强关联材料中实现拓扑量子相开辟了新的途径。

Abstract: Strong electron correlations and band topology each generate rich quantum phases, but conflicting elemental requirements have largely kept them apart. Topological polarization offers a route to unite them, producing polar surface states from bonding charge without spin-orbit coupling and thereby extending band topology to correlated 3d transition-metal compounds. Here we demonstrate that epitaxial thin films of the narrow-gap insulator FeSb2 host metallic polar surface states of topological-polarization origin, governed by the strong correlations of the bulk. Nonreciprocal surface transport emerges only below the onset temperature of a correlation-driven reconstruction of the bulk Fe 3d orbital occupation, providing direct evidence of bulk-edge correspondence in a correlated topological system. Moreover, electrostatic gating drives this correlated surface across a quantum phase transition into a ferromagnetic or possibly altermagnetic state. Our results establish topological polarization as a design principle for correlated topological phases in a broad range of materials.

</details>


### [113] [Landscape of incompressible crystals of hard-core bosons on the square-kagome lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05927)
*Pratyay Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用超越常规单胞描述的分层平均场理论研究了方kagome晶格上的硬核玻色子模型，发现常规单胞只能捕捉到密度为5/6和2/3的两个紧致局域态相，而通过扩大变分团簇后揭示了更多不可压缩态，其中ρ=3/4相最为稳健，对应自旋1/2 XXZ模型的半磁化平台。将该方法应用于两种实验相关的方kagome化合物，计算结果与实验磁化过程吻合良好并预测了额外平台结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规基于最小单胞的方kagome晶格描述无法完整捕捉该晶格所支持的不可压缩态层级，需要通过超越单胞的分层平均场理论来揭示更丰富的量子相结构，并连接实验相关材料。

Method: 采用分层平均场理论（hierarchical mean-field theory），通过扩大变分团簇至超越最小单胞的尺度，对方kagome晶格上的硬核玻色子模型进行系统研究。利用Matsubara-Matsuda映射将玻色子模型联系到自旋1/2 XXZ模型，并将方法应用于从第一性原理计算获得交换参数的真实材料。

Result: 常规单胞描述仅能复现密度为5/6和2/3的两个紧致局域态相；扩大变分团簇后发现额外不可压缩态，其中ρ=3/4相最为稳健；对两种实验相关方kagome化合物（基于第一性原理交换参数）的计算结果与现有实验数据吻合良好，并预测了额外磁化平台结构。

Conclusion: 超越最小单胞的分层平均场理论能够揭示方kagome晶格更完整的不可压缩态谱系，为理解相关磁性材料的磁化平台现象提供了新的理论框架和预测能力。

Abstract: We investigate a hard-core boson model on the square-kagome lattice using hierarchical mean-field theory beyond the conventional unit-cell description. We show that the conventional description of the square-kagome lattice based on its smallest unit cell does not fully capture the hierarchy of incompressible states supported by the lattice, but only captures the two compact-localized-state-based phases at densities $5/6$ and $2/3$. Within our approach, we reproduce these previously established phases and uncover additional incompressible states enabled by enlarging the variational cluster. Among these, the $ρ=3/4$ phase is found to be particularly robust, which, through the Matsubara-Matsuda mapping, corresponds to a half-magnetization plateau in the spin-$1/2$ XXZ model. We further apply our approach to two experimentally relevant square-kagome compounds using exchange parameters obtained from first-principles calculations. The calculated magnetization processes are in good agreement with available experimental results and predict additional plateau structures in these materials.

</details>


### [114] [Dissipation-induced bulk and boundary criticality in the Haldane chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05984)
*Zhenjiu Wang,Manuel Weber*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模量子蒙特卡洛模拟研究Haldane链耦合耗散欧姆浴的量子相变，发现二阶量子相变进入自发破缺SO(3)对称的反铁磁态，由动力学指数z≈2的相互作用不动点控制。提出了广义弦序参量，表明Haldane链的对称保护拓扑（SPT）基态在弱耗散下稳定，并在临界点具有非平凡标度维数。拓扑边缘模表现出不同于平庸态等价转变的边界临界性，后者通过耗散φ⁴理论的ε展开进行了精确计算。该体系实现了临界非共形反铁磁体中的自旋杂质，为耗散控制的可连续调谐边界临界性铺平了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究开放量子系统中对称保护拓扑相的稳定性和临界行为，特别是耗散对拓扑量子相变及边界临界性的影响，探索通过耗散实现可调谐的边界临界现象。

Method: 采用大规模量子蒙特卡洛模拟研究Haldane链耦合Ohmic耗散浴的量子相变；推广弦序参量表征SPT态稳定性；对平庸态的等价转变采用耗散φ⁴理论进行ε展开以获得解析结果。

Result: 发现二阶量子相变进入自发破缺SO(3)对称的反铁磁态，由动力学指数z≈2的相互作用不动点控制；弱耗散下SPT基态稳定，临界点具有非平凡标度维数；Haldane链的拓扑边缘模表现出与平庸态不同的边界临界性。

Conclusion: 耗散可以实现对边界临界性的连续调控，实现了临界非共形反铁磁体中的自旋杂质，为理解开放量子系统中拓扑物质与耗散的相互作用提供了新范式。

Abstract: We study the Haldane chain coupled to a dissipative ohmic bath. Using large-scale quantum Monte Carlo simulations, we identify a second-order quantum phase transition into an antiferromagnetic state with spontaneously broken SO(3) symmetry that is governed by an interacting fixed point with dynamical exponent $z\approx 2$. We derive a generalized string order parameter which indicates that the symmetry-protected topological ground state of the Haldane chain is stable for weak dissipation and develops a nontrivial scaling dimension at criticality. In particular, its topological edge modes display nontrivial boundary criticality that is distinct from an equivalent transition out of a trivial state; for the latter, we perform an accurate $ε$ expansion of a dissipative $φ^4$ theory with ordinary boundary conditions. Our setup realizes a spin impurity in a critical yet nonconformal antiferromagnet and paves the way for continuously tunable boundary criticality controlled by dissipation.

</details>


### [115] [Resilient strange metal at an unconventional quantum critical point in $d=2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05988)
*Jérôme Leblanc,A. -M. S. Tremblay*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过非微扰改进的双粒子自洽方法（TPSC）预测，二维Hubbard模型中即使在Mott转变以下，量子临界点（费米液体与非公度自旋密度波序之间的相变点）附近也会出现非常规临界指数。当关联长度从约一个到一百个晶格间距时，费米面上的Kohn反常导致强动量依赖的自能，且没有清晰的Landau准粒子证据，但电阻率仍呈线性温度依赖的奇异金属行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷原子实验和图表量子蒙特卡罗方法使得二维Hubbard模型的近期研究成为可能，但需要理论预测来阐明奇异金属行为及其与量子临界性质的关系。作者希望澄清线性温度电阻率是否必然源于费米面上自能的线性温度依赖，并探索量子临界点附近的非常规临界行为。

Method: 采用非微扰的改进双粒子自洽方法（improved two-particle self-consistent approach, TPSC）对最近邻跳跃的二维Hubbard模型进行数值预测，研究从约一个到一百个晶格间距的关联长度范围内的临界行为。

Result: 在费米液体与非公度自旋密度波序之间的量子临界点附近，费米面下方的Kohn反常导致非常规临界指数；单粒子自能沿费米面表现出强动量依赖且缺乏清晰的Landau准粒子特征。然而，电阻率仍随温度线性缩放（T→0），即表现出奇异金属行为。这表明线性温度电阻率可以出现，即使费米面上自能并非线性温度依赖。

Conclusion: 该工作证明线性温度依赖的电阻率并不必须来源于费米面上自能的线性温度依赖，打破了这一常见假设，为理解奇异金属行为与量子临界的关联提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Properties of the two-dimensional Hubbard model with nearest-neighbor hopping are under scrutiny in cold-atom experiments and diagrammatic quantum Monte Carlo. Given the controlled nature of these approaches, it is timely to make predictions about strange-metal behavior and its relation to quantum-critical properties. Even for interaction strengths below the Mott transition, several peculiarities occur at the quantum critical point separating Fermi liquid and incommensurate spin-density wave order. Here, we predict, using the non-perturbative improved two-particle self-consistent approach, that for correlation lengths ranging from about one to one hundred lattice spacings, Kohn anomalies on the underlying Fermi surface lead to unconventional critical exponents. The temperature dependence of the single-particle self-energy acquires strong momentum dependence along the Fermi surface with no clear evidence of Landau quasiparticles. Nevertheless, we observe strange-metal behavior, namely, resistivity that scales linearly with temperature as $T\rightarrow{0}$. Our work shows that a linear temperature dependence of resistivity arises without a linear-in-temperature self-energy along the Fermi surface, as is often assumed.

</details>


### [116] [Neural Flux Attachment: From Bose Condensates to Chiral Topological Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06168)
*Rudik Badalyan,Khachatur G. Nazaryan,Tigran A. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出ChernFormer，一种结合费米子transformer与固定Chern-Simons相位的神经波函数，可同时描述玻色凝聚体与手征拓扑液体（如Kalmeyer-Laughlin基态及其手征边缘态）。固定相位改变统计性质但不改变概率，使每个玻色子学习问题等价于费米子问题。数值实验表明其重叠曲线在最大取样尺寸下仍接近1，能恢复局域Laughlin涡旋和集体边缘涡旋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单个神经波函数难以同时描述常规玻色序（如玻色凝聚）与手征拓扑物质（如手征自旋液体）。需要一种统一的变分语言，既能表达无节点的凝聚波函数，又能表达具有手征边缘态和涡旋结构的拓扑波函数。

Method: ChernFormer将费米子transformer与固定Chern-Simons相位耦合：该相位为每对粒子附着一个统计涡旋，每个因子交换时改变符号，乘积精确为玻色子。由于固定相位只改变统计性质不改变概率，玻色子学习问题可等价转化为费米子问题。通过增加网络容量，可逼近任意正规化玻色子波函数。

Result: 在Kalmeyer-Laughlin基态及前两个手征边缘态上测试：重叠曲线在最大取样尺寸下仍接近1；N=20时独立振幅与相位图均能恢复局域Laughlin涡旋和集体边缘涡旋。相比之下，连续非零的逐粒子因子乘积无法捕捉这些基本边缘扇区。

Conclusion: ChernFormer为常规玻色序与手征拓扑物质提供了统一的变分描述语言，验证了单一种经网络架构能够同时表达凝聚体波函数和拓扑手征液体。

Abstract: Can one neural wave function describe both a Bose condensate and a chiral topological liquid? We introduce ChernFormer, which combines a fermionic transformer with a fixed Chern-Simons phase that attaches one statistical vortex to every particle pair. Each factor changes sign under exchange, so their product is exactly bosonic. The fixed phase changes statistics but not probability, making every bosonic learning problem equivalent to a fermionic one with the same approximation error and overlap. With enough capacity, ChernFormer can approximate any normalizable bosonic wave function on the plane at fixed particle number. A finite, smooth network still vanishes when particles meet, yet this contact hole can shrink while the wave function and condensate fraction approach those of a nodeless condensate. Following the needle-in-a-haystack target-reconstruction benchmark introduced in \cite{NazaryanGaggioliTengFu2025}, we test ChernFormer on the Kalmeyer--Laughlin ground state and its first two chiral edge states. The overlap curves stay close to unity through their largest sampled sizes, while independent amplitude and phase maps at $N=20$ for all three states recover both local Laughlin vortices and the collective edge vortex. By contrast, a continuous, nonzero product of identical particle-wise factors misses these elementary edge sectors. ChernFormer therefore provides one variational language for conventional bosonic order and chiral topological matter.

</details>


### [117] [Phonon spectral functions of low-density polaron metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06357)
*Luis Walther,Alberto Nocera,Mona Berciu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）计算了低浓度掺杂（x≤0.15）的一维无自旋Holstein模型的声子谱函数，发现显著的声子谱权重转移到裸声子能量以下（直至ω=0）和以上，而非Migdal极限中预期的Kohn反常行为。RPA和"dressed RPA"方案（将电子传播子用Momentum Average近似重整化）能定性乃至半定量地捕捉该行为，而最低阶顶点修正会产生非物理的负谱权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的声子谱研究主要集中在单极化子极限（x→0），缺乏低但有限载流子浓度下的结果。作者希望填补这一空白，理解弱掺杂极化子金属中电子-声子耦合对声子谱函数的影响，这一区域与弱掺杂绝缘体物理相关。

Method: 采用DMRG数值方法计算一维无自旋Holstein模型在低浓度掺杂（x≤0.15）下的声子谱函数，作为电子-声子耦合λ的函数。将DMRG结果与RPA和"dressed RPA"（在RPA基础上用Momentum Average近似重整化电子加添传播子）进行比较，并检验加入最低阶顶点修正的效果。

Result: 发现显著的声子谱权重同时转移到裸声子能量以下（直至ω=0）和以上，对比Migdal极限中预期的Kohn反常（权重集中在Ω附近且在q=2k_F处出现扭结），结果中未出现2k_F扭结的特征。RPA能定性再现该行为，"dressed RPA"可半定量地捕捉，且计算成本可忽略；但加入最低阶顶点修正会产生负声子谱权重这一非物理结果。

Conclusion: 一旦传播子被非微扰地重整化，顶点和传播子必须一致地处理，否则会产生非物理结果。"dressed RPA"为低密度极化子金属的声子谱函数提供了高效近似，适用于弱掺杂绝缘体区域。

Abstract: We use the density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) to compute the phonon spectral function of a one-dimensional spinless Holstein model doped with a low but finite carrier concentration, $x \leq 0.15$, as a function of the electron-phonon coupling $λ$. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first such results in this regime, complementing extensive prior work at the single-polaron level ($x\to 0$). We find that significant phonon spectral weight is transferred both below, all the way down to $ω=0$, and above the bare phonon energy $Ω$, in stark contrast with the Kohn-anomaly phenomenology expected in the Migdal limit, where weight remains centered near $Ω$ with a kink at $q=2k_F$. No signature of this $2k_F$ kink appears in our results. This behavior is captured qualitatively by the Random Phase Approximation (RPA), and semi-quantitatively, at negligible extra computational cost, by a ``dressed RPA'' scheme in which the electron addition propagator is renormalized using the Momentum Average (MA) approximation for the low-density electron-polaron. By contrast, adding the lowest-order vertex correction to this dressed scheme produces unphysical negative spectral weight, signaling that vertex and propagator dressings must be treated consistently once the propagators are dressed nonperturbatively. Our results provide an efficient approximation for the phonon spectral functions of low-density polaron metals, a regime relevant to weakly doped insulators.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Soprano voices in opera seria: a corpus-based inquiry into eighteenth-century vocal types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05257)
*Ana Llorens,Eduardo García-Portugués,Carlos Vaquero,Álvaro Torrente*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过统计模型分析约1,700首十八世纪意大利正歌剧咏叹调，发现作曲家风格的编码主要反映角色性别而非歌手性别：男性角色咏叹调具有大幅旋律跳进、音程变化大和音域宽广的特征，女性角色则偏爱高音、小音程和旋律稳定。歌手性别对音乐写作的影响几乎不可恢复，女性歌手音高更高主要与角色塑造相关而非生理因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十八世纪意大利正歌剧中女高音声部占主导，跨性别选角（cross-casting）普遍存在，形成了四种表演者性别与角色性别组合的配置。作者假设作曲家会根据首演歌手的特质以及角色的性别来量身定制咏叹调写作，因此希望量化验证这一假设。

Method: 基于约1,700首咏叹调构建统计模型，分析声乐类型、角色性别与音乐风格选择之间的关系。对比多种监督学习方法，最终选用岭回归逻辑回归（ridge logistic regression）作为主要模型，因其在性能上可与最佳替代方法竞争，同时允许对系数进行解释和不确定性量化。

Result: 结果揭示了不对称性：角色性别在音乐写作中被持续一致地编码，而歌手性别几乎无法从音乐中恢复。男性角色咏叹调具有大跳进、音程变异性和更宽的音域；女性角色咏叹调偏爱更高的最高音、小音程和旋律稳定性。女歌手比男高音歌手唱得更高，但这更多与角色刻画相关而非生理差异。

Conclusion: 作曲家创作中角色性别是核心的风格编码维度，而歌手生理性别的影响微弱；声乐音高差异主要由戏剧角色规定而非歌手生理条件决定。

Abstract: Eighteenth-century Italian opera seria was dominated by soprano voices. Although masculine roles were typically performed by castrati and feminine roles by women, cross-casting was common, producing four soprano configurations in which performers of either sex could portray characters of either gender. We hypothesize that composers tailored their writing both to the singers premiering their arias and to the characters' gender. Using statistical models built on approximately 1,700 arias, we analyze relationships between vocal typology, characters' gender, and stylistic choices, comparing supervised learning methods and selecting ridge logistic regression as our primary model, whose performance is competitive with that of the best-performing alternative while allowing for interpretation and uncertainty quantification of its coefficients. Results reveal an asymmetry: character gender is consistently encoded in the writing, while the singer's sex is barely recoverable. Masculine-character arias feature large melodic leaps, intervallic variability, and wider ranges; feminine-character arias favor higher maximum pitches, minor intervals, and melodic stability. Female singers sang higher pitches than male sopranos, but this correlates more with character portrayal than with physiology.

</details>


### [119] [Conditional multivariate functional PCA for the reconstruction of temperature and salinity profiles partially sampled by deep-diving marine mammals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05376)
*Nadège Fonvieille,Christophe Guinet,Baptiste Picard,David Nerini*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多元函数主成分分析（PCA）的统计方法，用于重建南印度洋扇区南象海豹生物记录仪采集的部分采样温度和盐度垂直剖面。通过参数化估计完整双变量剖面的协方差结构和均值函数构建特征函数基，利用条件估计将不完整剖面投影到特征空间完成重建。模拟实验表明，加入地理协变量后，250米截断剖面在20-500米范围内的重建精度对温度和盐度分别提升30%和33%。方法成功重建了约9万个不完整剖面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 南象海豹携带的生物记录仪为南大洋提供了前所未有的时空覆盖数据，但动物的潜水行为导致最大记录深度随行为变化，只能提供垂直环境的局部视图。需要一种统计方法从部分采样的温盐剖面中重建完整的垂直热盐条件。

Method: 采用多变量泛函主成分分析（FPCA）：(1) 从一组完整双变量剖面中参数化估计协方差结构和均值函数，构建特征函数基；(2) 考虑测量误差方差，通过条件估计不完整剖面的泛函主成分坐标；(3) 将部分采样的温度和盐度剖面投影到完整剖面的特征空间中，在定义域上重建。同时纳入地理协变量提升重建精度。

Result: 模拟实验中，截断深度为250米、重建范围为20-500米的剖面，加入地理协变量后，温度重建精度提升30%，盐度提升33%。在实际数据中，利用约1万个到达500米的完整剖面，重建了约9万个不完整剖面，覆盖法国亚南极岛屿周边约300万平方公里区域。

Conclusion: 基于多元函数PCA的条件估计方法能有效重建部分采样的温盐垂直剖面，地理协变量的引入显著提升重建精度，该方法可充分挖掘生物记录仪数据中未达深层的剖面信息，扩展南大洋热盐条件的时空覆盖。

Abstract: We present a statistical method to reconstruct the vertical thermohaline conditions in the Indian Sector of the Southern Ocean, where temperature and salinity profiles are partially sampled by female southern elephant seals. Datasets collected by biologgers provide unprecedented spatial and temporal coverage of ocean conditions. However, the maximum recorded depth varies with the animals' behaviour, offering only a partial view of the vertical environment. Using multivariate functional Principal Component Analysis (PCA), a parametric estimation of the covariance structure and mean function from a set of complete bivariate profiles allows the construction of an eigenfunction basis. By accounting for measurement error variance, partially sampled temperature and salinity profiles can be projected into the eigenspace of the complete profiles through conditional estimation of their functional principal coordinates and then reconstructed over the defined domain. For simulated snippet profiles truncated at depth $z_{\max} = 250$ m and reconstructed over $\mathcal{Z} = [20,500]$ m, reconstruction accuracy increases by 30 % for temperature and 33 % for salinity when incorporating geographical covariates. We then reconstruct ~90,000 incomplete profiles from the multivariate functional PCA of ~10,000 profiles reaching 500 m, covering approximately 3 million km$^2$ around the French subantarctic islands.

</details>


### [120] [Modeling E-Bike Route Choice in Washington, DC: A Path Size Logit Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05449)
*Yiheng Qian,Zehui Yin,Darren M. Scott,Xiang Yan*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本研究利用华盛顿特区Capital Bikeshare系统的GPS轨迹数据，结合GIS基础设施变量与街景图像（SVI）的计算机视觉特征，构建路径规模Logit模型分析共享电动自行车路径选择行为，发现骑行者在规避机动车与行人冲突的同时追求行程连续性，自行车设施的作用受道路等级调节，且视觉特征（尤其是树木）对模型有增强效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动自行车路径选择对于有效的骑行基础设施规划至关重要，但相关的实证证据仍然有限，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用华盛顿特区Capital Bikeshare系统的GPS轨迹数据，构建混合选择集（含观测路径与对应最短路径），采用路径规模Logit（Path Size Logit）模型，整合GIS基础设施变量和从街景图像（SVI）提取的计算机视觉街道级视觉特征。

Result: 电动自行车骑行者倾向于选择能最小化与机动车和行人冲突且保持行程连续性的路线；道路等级显著调节自行车设施的作用，主干道上自行车设施影响远大于次要道路；较长行程对骑行基础设施表现出更强偏好；街景视觉特征（尤其树木）能提升模型性能，但影响普遍小于道路基础设施；标准化效应量识别出行为上最重要的路线属性。

Conclusion: 研究为电动自行车时代的骑行基础设施规划提供了实践性证据，强调了道路等级与自行车设施交互作用的重要性，以及街道绿植（特别是树木）对路径选择的积极影响。

Abstract: Understanding e-bike route choice is essential for developing effective cycling infrastructure, yet empirical evidence remains limited. This study investigates shared e-bike route choice in Washington, DC, using Global Positioning System (GPS) trajectory data from the Capital Bikeshare system. A Path Size Logit model is estimated using a hybrid choice set consisting of observed routes and corresponding shortest paths, integrating Geographic Information System (GIS)-based infrastructure variables with computer vision-derived street-level visual features extracted from Street View images (SVI). The results indicate that e-bike riders tend to choose routes that minimize conflicts with both motor vehicles and pedestrians while maintaining travel continuity. Roadway hierarchy substantially moderates the influence of bicycle facilities, with the presence of bicycle facilities having a much greater impact on route choice along major roads than along minor roads. Longer trips also exhibit stronger preferences for cycling infrastructure. Incorporating street-level visual features improves model performance, although their effects are generally smaller than those of road infrastructure, with trees being the only greenery component showing a consistently positive effect. Standardized effect sizes further identify the most behaviorally important route attributes. These findings provide practical evidence for cycling infrastructure planning in the e-bike era.

</details>


### [121] [How Infrastructure and Streetscape Shape E-Scooter Route Choice: Evidence from Washington, DC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05465)
*Yiheng Qian,Duanya Lyu,Wenwen Zhang,Steve Hankey,Meng Qi,Xiang Yan*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本研究利用华盛顿特区的GPS轨迹数据和Path Size Logit模型，探讨电动滑板车的路径选择行为。研究发现自行车设施的有效性取决于道路环境：主干道上只有受保护的自行车设施能提升路线吸引力，而次干道上受保护和划线车道均有增益。人行道是最常用的骑行环境，但只有沥青铺设的人行道与正效用相关。道路基础设施比视觉街景特征对路径选择影响更大，但后者仍提供额外解释力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动滑板车已成为短途城市出行的重要微出行方式，但关于其路线选择行为的实证证据仍然有限，需要进行系统研究以填补这一空白。

Method: 使用华盛顿特区的GPS轨迹数据，构建Path Size Logit模型，融合道路和基础设施特征，并利用计算机视觉从Google Street View图像中提取视觉街景特征纳入模型分析。

Result: 自行车设施效果依赖道路情境：主干道仅受保护设施有效，次干道受保护和划线车道均有效；人行道是使用最多的骑行环境但仅沥青路面有正效用；树木覆盖（尤其夏季）和建筑/墙体覆盖与路线选择正相关；道路基础设施比视觉街景的影响更强，但后者有额外解释力。

Conclusion: 研究结果为有针对性的基础设施投资提供依据，建议将街景改善作为提升微出行路线吸引力的补充策略。

Abstract: E-scooters have emerged as an important micromobility mode for short urban trips, yet evidence on route choice behavior remains limited. This study examines e-scooter route choice in Washington, DC using GPS trajectory data and a Path Size Logit model. In addition to roadway and infrastructure characteristics, the model incorporates visual streetscape features extracted from Google Street View imagery using computer vision techniques. The results show that the effectiveness of cycling infrastructure depends strongly on roadway context. On major roads, only protected bicycle facilities significantly increase route attractiveness, whereas on minor roads both protected and designated lanes provide utility gains. Sidewalks constitute the most frequently used riding environment, yet only asphalt-paved sidewalks are associated with positive utility, suggesting that sidewalk riding may reflect the absence of attractive on-street alternatives rather than a preference for pedestrian infrastructure. Tree coverage, particularly during summer, as well as building and wall coverage, are positively associated with route choice. Likelihood ratio tests and value-of-distance analysis indicate that roadway infrastructure exerts a stronger influence on route choice than visual streetscape features, although the latter provide additional explanatory power. These findings support targeted infrastructure investment and the integration of streetscape improvements as a complementary strategy for enhancing micromobility route attractiveness.

</details>


### [122] [Long-Term Mortality Following STN-DBS in Parkinson's Disease: A Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05609)
*Malinda Iluppangama,Dilmi Abeywardana*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本研究对214名接受丘脑底核深部脑刺激（STN-DBS）的帕金森病患者进行了回顾性生存分析，发现中位生存期约为134个月，5年、10年、15年死亡率分别为8.0%、42.5%和56.0%。植入年龄≥60岁显著增加死亡风险（HR=4.689），而术后至少一次修正手术显著降低死亡风险（HR=0.296），且两种结果均由Cox比例风险模型和加速失败时间模型双重验证。患者满意度高达97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STN-DBS是帕金森病已确立的治疗方法，能改善运动症状和生活质量，但长期生存率及死亡风险因素尚未得到充分表征，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对214名接受STN-DBS的帕金森病患者进行回顾性生存分析。采用Kaplan-Meier方法估计生存概率和中位生存期，用log-rank检验比较不同性别和植入年龄组的生存差异；使用Cox比例风险模型在单变量和多变量分析中识别死亡相关因素，并通过加速失败时间（AFT）模型进行验证。

Result: 死亡率随时间进行性上升：5年、10年和15年内死亡率分别为8.0%、42.5%和56.0%，中位生存期约为134个月。性别间生存差异不显著（p=0.63），但植入年龄≥60岁的患者生存率显著更低（p<0.0001）。术后至少一次修正手术降低死亡风险（HR=0.296, p<0.001），植入年龄≥60岁增加死亡风险（HR=4.689, p<0.001），两者在校正后仍显著且经AFT模型确认。52名受访患者中97%表示满意，96%愿意推荐STN-DBS。

Conclusion: STN-DBS与帕金森病患者的长期生存和高患者满意度相关。植入年龄较大增加死亡风险，而修正手术与死亡率之间的关系可能反映的是适应证混杂而非直接因果效应。研究结果支持按年龄分层进行患者筛选，并强调了STN-DBS术后长期持续监测的重要性。

Abstract: Background: Deep brain stimulation of the subthalamic nucleus (STN-DBS) is an established PD treatment that improves motor symptoms and quality of life, but long-term survival and mortality risk remain poorly characterized. This study evaluated long-term survival and mortality-associated factors after STN-DBS.
  Methods: A retrospective survival analysis of 214 PD patients who underwent STN-DBS used Kaplan-Meier methods to estimate survival probabilities and median survival, with log-rank tests comparing survival by gender and age at implantation. Cox proportional hazards (Cox-PH) models identified mortality-associated factors in both univariate and multivariate analyses, and were validated using accelerated failure time (AFT) models.
  Results: Mortality rose progressively, with 8.0%, 42.5%, and 56.0% of patients dying within 5, 10, and 15 years, respectively; median survival was approximately 134 months. Survival did not differ by gender (p = 0.63) but was significantly lower for patients aged 60 years or older at implantation (p < 0.0001). At least one post-implantation revision reduced mortality risk (HR = 0.296, p < 0.001), while implantation at age 60 years or older increased it (HR = 4.689, p < 0.001); both remained significant after adjustment and were confirmed by AFT models. Patient satisfaction was high, with 97% of 52 surveyed patients reporting satisfaction and 96% would recommend STN-DBS.
  Conclusion: STN-DBS is linked to long-term survival and high patient satisfaction in individuals with PD. Older age at implantation increased mortality risk, whereas revision surgery and mortality likely reflect confounding by indication rather than a direct causal effect. These findings support age-stratified patient selection and highlight the importance of continued long-term monitoring following STN-DBS.

</details>


### [123] [Information leakage from data revisions in retrospective forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05883)
*Johannes Bracher,Sebastian Funk*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文指出Aygün等人声称其AI驱动的ERA系统在2024/25流感季COVID-19住院预测中大幅优于CDC集合预报，但作者证明这一性能优势源于回顾性预测设置中的信息泄漏——因为数据修订未被考虑。类似机制在许多预测领域普遍存在，该警示不仅适用于流行病预测，也适用于整个新兴的AI辅助预测建模领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证并质疑Aygün等人声称其ERA系统在COVID-19住院预测中显著优于CDC集合预报的结果，揭示其性能优势可能并非源于AI系统本身的预测能力，而是源自回顾性预测设置中的方法缺陷。

Method: 通过分析回顾性预测设置中数据修订未纳入考量所导致的信息泄漏机制，证明Aygün等人观察到的性能优势是人为产物，并指出该机制在许多预测领域同样存在。

Result: 证明了ERA系统观察到的性能优势可归因于回顾性预测设置中的信息泄漏（数据修订未考虑），而非真实的预测能力提升。

Conclusion: 该警示具有广泛适用性：信息泄漏机制不仅存在于流行病预测中，对整个人工智能辅助预测建模领域都有重要参考价值，提醒研究者在评估AI预测系统时必须谨慎处理数据修订等回顾性偏差问题。

Abstract: Aygün et al (2026, https://doi.org/10.1038/s41586-026-10658-6) claim that their AI-driven Empirical Research Assistance (ERA) system produces COVID-19 hospitalisation forecasts which outperform the state-of-the-art CDC ensemble by a considerable margin for the 2024/25 season. We demonstrate that the observed performance gain is attributable to information leakage in the retrospective forecasting setup, which resulted because data revisions were not taken into account. As similar mechanisms are at play in many other forecasting fields, our cautionary tale applies not just to epidemic forecasting, but is relevant to the entire emerging field of AI-assisted predictive modelling.

</details>


### [124] [Evaluating the influence of treatment-effect heterogeneity on discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06002)
*Florie Bouvier,Etienne Peyrot,Francois Petit,Raphaël Porcher*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了个体化治疗效果的异质性分析中，条件平均治疗效果（CATE）模型在不同分布下所能达到的理论判别能力上限。作者在20种不同平均治疗效果和异质性水平的分布上，计算了三种判别指标（benefit的c-statistic、benefit集中度和群体平均处方效应PAPE）在"神谕CATE"下的理论表现。结果显示三种指标对异质性水平的要求不同：达到高的c-statistic和PAPE需要更强的异质性，而benefit集中度在异质性可忽略的情况下也能指示完美判别，三种指标在不同分布下表现差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量方法用于开发CATE模型和个体化治疗规则，但关于根据人群治疗效果的基础分布能达到何种判别能力，学界知之甚少。理解CATE模型判别能力的理论上限对于评估模型性能和指导模型选择至关重要。

Method: 在20种具有不同平均治疗效果和异质性水平的分布上，计算"神谕CATE"（oracle CATE）下的判别能力，评估三种指标：benefit的c-statistic、benefit集中度（concentration of benefit）以及群体平均处方效应（PAPE，population average prescriptive effect）。

Result: 三种指标对治疗效果异质性的要求不同：获得高c-statistic for benefit和PAPE值需要较异质性更强的治疗效果分布，而benefit集中度在治疗效果异质性可忽略时也能达到完美判别。三种指标在不同分布下行为差异显著。

Conclusion: 不同判别指标对治疗效果异质性水平的敏感度不同，实际应用中选择判别指标时应考虑人群治疗效果的基础分布特征，以避免对模型判别能力产生误导性评估。

Abstract: Analyzing the heterogeneity of treatment effects is crucial in personalized medicine to identify which patients will benefit from specific treatments. The performance of a conditional average treatment effects model to guide treatment decisions can be assessed in different ways, with an important one being the model's ability to effectively discriminate between individuals who benefit from the treatment and those who do not. While many methods and algorithms have been proposed to develop conditional average treatment effects models and individualized treatment rules, little is known about the discriminative ability that can be achieved according to the population's underlying distribution of treatment effects. In this work, we computed the discrimination that can be achieved under oracle CATE for a panel of 20 distributions with varying average treatment effects and levels of heterogeneity. The assessment included the following discrimination metrics: the c-statistic for benefit, the concentration of benefit, and the population average prescriptive effect (PAPE). Results showed that the three metrics employed in this study did not require the same levels of treatment effect heterogeneity to lead to high discrimination results. Notably, achieving high c-statistic for benefit and PAPE values required greater heterogeneity than obtaining high concentration of benefit values. The three metrics considered behave very differently across the distributions. For instance, the concentration of benefit can indicate perfect discrimination in settings with negligible treatment-effects heterogeneity.

</details>


### [125] [Longitudinal wearable monitoring and polygenic risk for incident major depressive disorder in the All of Us Research Program](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06063)
*Yuezhou Zhang,Amos A. Folarin,Rongrong Zhong,Hyunju Kim,Shaoxiong Sun,Callum Stewart,Richard JB Dobson*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本研究整合了基因组、电子健康记录（EHR）和纵向Fitbit可穿戴设备数据，来自All of Us研究计划中3,030名欧洲血统成人（其中284例在基线后180天发生EHR记录的MDD），使用时变Cox模型发现MDD多基因风险评分（PRS）、较低每日步数、较低轻度和剧烈体力活动、较低睡眠效率及较大睡眠时长变异性均与MDD发病风险增加相关；久坐时间和睡眠时长变异性与MDD发病的关联因PRS水平而异，高PRS者的关联更强。整合PRS、基线及月度的可穿戴特征及交互项后，C指数从0.637提升至0.705。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重度抑郁症（MDD）的风险同时反映稳定的遗传易感性和动态行为模式，但以往研究很少在真实世界环境中使用长期客观数据将这两个维度结合起来考察。作者旨在利用基因组、EHR和长期可穿戴设备数据，探究遗传易感性与纵向行为特征如何共同影响MDD的发病风险。

Method: 采用All of Us研究计划中3,030名欧洲血统成人（284例在180天基线期后发生EHR记录的MDD），使用时变Cox模型分析MDD PRS、月度可穿戴设备衍生的体力活动和睡眠特征，以及可穿戴特征与MDD PRS之间的交互作用与MDD发病的关联。使用PRS分层危险比曲线比较不同遗传风险水平下的行为-风险关系，并通过逐步整合模型评估判别性能（C指数）。

Result: 较高MDD PRS、较低每日步数、较低轻度和剧烈体力活动、较低睡眠效率和较大睡眠时长变异性与MDD发病风险增加相关。久坐时间和睡眠时长变异性与MDD发病的关联在不同PRS水平下存在差异，高PRS参与者中风险关联更强。PRS分层曲线显示，高PRS参与者达到可比估计风险需要更有利的行为水平。逐步整合PRS、基线可穿戴特征、月度可穿戴特征及交互项使C指数从0.637提升至0.705。

Conclusion: 研究支持遗传易感性和纵向真实世界行为监测对MDD发病风险表征的互补价值，为未来基于基因信息的数字表型分析用于个性化风险监测和预防提供了依据。

Abstract: Major depressive disorder (MDD) risk reflects both stable inherited liability and dynamic behavioral patterns, yet these dimensions are rarely examined together using long-term objective data in real-world settings. Here, we integrated genomic, electronic health record (EHR), and longitudinal Fitbit wearable data from 3,030 adults of genetically inferred European ancestry in the All of Us Research Program, 284 of whom developed EHR-recorded incident MDD after a 180-day baseline period. Time-varying Cox models examined associations of MDD polygenic risk scores (PRS), monthly wearable-derived physical activity and sleep features, and interactions between wearable features and MDD PRS with incident MDD. Higher MDD PRS, lower daily steps, lower light and vigorous physical activity, lower sleep efficiency, and greater sleep duration variability were associated with higher risk of EHR-recorded incident MDD. The associations of sedentary time and sleep duration variability with incident MDD differed across MDD PRS levels, with stronger risk associations among participants with higher MDD PRS. PRS-stratified hazard ratio curves further indicated that comparable estimated risk corresponded to more favorable behavioral levels (such as higher daily step counts and more stable sleep) among participants with higher MDD PRS than among those with lower MDD PRS. Sequentially integrating MDD PRS, baseline wearable features, monthly wearable features, and selected interactions between wearable features and MDD PRS increased model discrimination, with the C-index increasing from 0.637 to 0.705. These findings support the complementary value of inherited liability and longitudinal real-world behavioral monitoring for incident MDD risk characterization and may inform future work on genetically informed digital phenotyping for personalized risk monitoring and prevention.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Rigidity of Averages over the Two Largest Prime Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05191)
*Dijia Chen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个数论问题：若对质数集上的有界函数f，其作用于n的最大素因子P1(n)的平均值收敛，则作用于第二大的不同素因子P2(n)的平均值必然收敛到同一极限，回答了Alladi和Johnson提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alladi和Johnson询问是否存在一个定义在素数上的有界函数f，使得其分别作用于n的最大不同素因子P1(n)和第二大不同素因子P2(n)的均值极限存在但不相等。本文旨在回答这个开放问题。

Method: 将对数对数尺度下的两个均值表示为与显式Dickman核的卷积。利用P1均值相关的Dickman核的傅里叶变换在实数轴上无零点这一性质，借助Wiener的Tauberian定理，得到加权素数和的平移测度的模糊收敛性，从而推导出第二个均值的收敛性。

Result: 证明了P1均值的收敛性会强制P2均值收敛且收敛到相同的极限，从而否定了Alladi-Johnson问题中两个极限不相等的情形存在的可能性。

Conclusion: Alladi-Johnson问题的答案是负面的：不存在这样的有界函数f使得两个均值极限存在且不相等；P1均值的收敛足以保证P2均值以相同极限收敛。

Abstract: Let \(P_1(n)\) and \(P_2(n)\) be the largest and second-largest distinct prime factors of \(n\), respectively. Alladi and Johnson asked whether there exists a bounded function \(f\) on the primes for which both limits \(\frac{1}{x}\sum_{2\le n\le x} f(P_1(n)) \longrightarrow κ_1\) and \(\frac{1}{x}\sum_{2\le n\le x} f(P_2(n)) \longrightarrow κ_2\) exist with \(κ_1\neqκ_2\), where we set \(f(P_2(n))=0\) when \(n\) is a prime power. We prove that this is impossible: convergence of the first average forces convergence of the second to the same limit.
  On the \(\log\log\)-scale, the two averages are expressed as convolutions with explicit Dickman kernels. The Fourier transform of the Dickman kernel associated with the \(P_1\)-average has no real zeros. Wiener's Tauberian theorem then yields vague convergence of the translated measures associated with the weighted prime sums. The convergence of the second average follows.

</details>


### [127] [Modularity of Point Counts for the Curves $X^a=Y^b$: New Rogers--Ramanujan Identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05480)
*Kenny Lau,Ken Ono*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Huang-Jiang-Oblomkov 关于交换幂零矩阵对计数猜想的 a=3 层：建立了一族新的 Rogers-Ramanujan 恒等式，并给出了 Warnaar 乘积的几何来源，同时用 Lean 定理证明器（AxiomProver）验证了这些恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该猜想将特定幂零矩阵对的计数（点计数）、欧拉 q-级数与 theta 函数商三者联系起来。a=2 层已有经典结果（Rogers-Ramanujan 和 Andrews-Gordon 恒等式），但 a≥3 层此前一无所知，本文目标是攻克 a=3 层。

Method: 通过建立 a=3 层的新无限族 Rogers-Ramanujan 恒等式，将 Z_{3,b}(q) 与显式乘积 P_{3,b}(q) 联系起来；同时利用几何方法解释 Warnaar 乘积的起源，并使用形式化证明工具 Lean（AxiomProver）在既有文献假设下验证这些新恒等式。

Result: 完整证明了 a=3 层猜想：得到了一族新的无限 Rogers-Ramanujan 类型恒等式，证明了点计数与模块形式（Γ(a+b) 上的模函数）的关系，并给出了 Warnaar 乘积的几何解释。所有新恒等式均通过 Lean 中的 AxiomProver 验证。

Conclusion: a=3 层的完整证明实现了该猜想在非经典层上的首次突破，为后续 a≥4 层的证明提供了方法论基础，并展示了形式化验证在 q-级数恒等式研究中的可行性。

Abstract: For coprime $1<a<b$, let $M_n^{a,b}(\mathbb{F}_q)$ be the set of commuting pairs of nilpotent $n\times n$ matrices over $\mathbb{F}_q$ with $X^a=Y^b$. Huang, Jiang, and Oblomkov assembled their orders as an Eulerian $q$-series
  $Z_{a,b}(q)$. They conjectured that it is an explicit product $P_{a,b}(q)$ involving Jacobi's theta function and Dedekind's eta-function, implying the threefold equality $$\underbrace{\prod_{n\ge1}(1-q^n)\cdot\Biggl(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{|M_n^{a,b}(\mathbb{F}_q)|}{|\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{F}_q)|}\Biggr)\Biggr|_{q\mapsto q^{-1}}}_{\text{point count}}\;=\;\underbrace{Z_{a,b}(q)}_{q\text{-series}}\;=\;\underbrace{P_{a,b}(q)}_{\text{theta quotient}}$$ If true, the point count on $X^a=Y^b$ is essentially a modular function on $Γ(a+b)$. The conjecture is layered in $a$, with an identity for each $b$. The $a=2$ layer is classical, including identities of Rogers--Ramanujan and Andrews--Gordon. For $a\geq3,$ nothing was known. We prove the $a=3$ layer in full: a new infinite family of Rogers--Ramanujan identities, and a geometric origin for Warnaar's products. AxiomProver verified these new identities in Lean assuming existing literature.

</details>


### [128] [Explicit Algebraic Numbers all whose Integer Parts of Powers are Composite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05309)
*Suyeong Hahn,Dan Ismailescu,Ganghun Kim,Minseung Kim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了多类代数整数 α>1，使得 ⌊α^n⌋（取整幂序列）在除有限个 n 之外的所有情形下均为合数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究取整幂序列 ⌊α^n⌋ 的素性/合数性质是数论中的经典问题。该工作旨在推广已有结果，构造更广泛的代数整数类，使得其取整幂序列几乎全部为合数。

Method: 通过构造显式的代数整数类（具体地给出 α 的代数表达式或满足的代数方程条件），并利用代数数论或初等数论的整除性/同余技巧，证明 ⌊α^n⌋ 对几乎所有 n 都有非平凡因子。

Result: 给出了若干显式的代数整数 α>1 的类，满足对所有但有限多个正整数 n，⌊α^n⌋ 均为合数。

Conclusion: 该结果丰富了取整幂序列素性问题的构造性成果，为进一步研究此类序列的数论性质提供了新的显式例子。

Abstract: We present several explicit classes of algebraic integers $α>1$ such that $\lfloor α^n\rfloor$ is composite for all but finitely many $n$.

</details>


### [129] [The sesquicentennial of the prime number $2^{127} - 1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05319)
*Matthew Baker*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文回顾了1876年卢卡斯证明39位数M_127=2^127-1为素数的历史，这是75年来最大的已知质数，也是无需机械装置发现与验证的最大素数。文章同时给出了现代证明的卢卡斯-莱默检验法，这种方法至今仍是验证大型素数的主要引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2026年将是卢卡斯这一杰出数学成就的150周年纪念，作者希望通过回顾历史并给出卢卡斯-莱默检验的现代证明，来致敬这一里程碑式的发现，并阐明其在现代计算数论中的持续重要性。

Method: 文章结合了历史叙述与数学证明：一方面梳理1876年卢卡斯发现M_127为素数的历史背景，另一方面对卢卡斯-莱默检验法提供现代严谨的数学证明。

Result: M_127是历史上最大的无需机械辅助设备发现和验证的素数，该纪录保持了75年。卢卡斯-莱默检验法至今仍是大型素数认证的核心算法。

Conclusion: 卢卡斯-莱默检验法作为近150年来素数验证领域的基石性方法，其数学原理简洁深邃，至今仍在大数据时代的素数认证中发挥着不可替代的作用。

Abstract: The year 2026 marks the 150th anniversary of a remarkable achievement in mathematics: in 1876, the French mathematician Édouard Lucas showed that the 39-digit number $M_{127} := 2^{127} -1$ is prime. This stood for 75 years as the largest known prime, and it remains the largest prime number discovered and certified without the aid of a mechanical device. We review the history behind this discovery and give a modern proof of the closely related Lucas-Lehmer test, which is still the main engine behind the certification of large prime numbers.

</details>


### [130] [Trianguline representations and locally analytic principal series of ${\rm GL}_2({\mathbb Q}_p)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05396)
*Matthias Strauch,Zichuan Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于p进Langlands对应的重要结果：若GL₂(Q_p)的酉Banach空间表示Π(ρ)所对应的局部解析表示有子商同构于局部解析主序列表示的非零子商，则相应的2维p进Galois表示ρ必须是可三角化的（trianguline）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立p进Langlands对应中局部解析表示的性质与Galois表示的结构之间的联系，具体地说是判断局部解析表示何时包含主序列表示的子商，以及这对Galois表示意味着什么。

Method: 利用Colmez、Dospinescu、Paškūnas、Emerton等人的已有结果，通过对局部解析表示的子商结构进行分析和推导。

Result: 证明了若Π(ρ)^la有子商同构于局部解析主序列表示的非零子商，则ρ是trianguline的。

Conclusion: 该结果深化了对p进Langlands对应中三角化Galois表示的刻画，将局部解析表示的结构性质转化为Galois表示的可三角化条件。

Abstract: Let $ρ$ be an absolutely irreducible 2-dimensional $p$-adic representation of the absolute Galois group of ${\mathbb Q}_p$, and let $Π(ρ)$ be the unitary Banach space representation of $G = {\rm GL}_2({\mathbb Q}_p)$ associated to $ρ$ by the $p$-adic Langlands correspondence. We deduce from results due to Colmez, Dospinescu, Paškūnas, Emerton, and others that if the locally analytic representation $Π(ρ)^\rm{la}$ has a subquotient isomorphic to a non-zero subquotient of a locally analytic principal series representation of $G$, then $ρ$ is trianguline.

</details>


### [131] [Asymptotic uncorrelations between functions with squarefull kernel and functions of invariant average](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05470)
*Xiang Su,Biao Wang,Shaoyun Yi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了s-函数（平方满核算术函数）及其移位卷积在唯一遍历拓扑动力系统中与沿prime Ω函数的轨道渐近不相关，从而得到了素数定理的一个精细化版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ivić和Tenenbaum在1986年引入了平方满核算术函数（s-函数），Erdős和Ivić对其移位卷积和给出了渐近估计。近年来，Bergelson和Richter在唯一遍历系统中沿着prime Ω函数的轨道建立了素数定理的新动力学推广。本文旨在将这些s-函数的研究与动力学系统中的轨道行为联系起来，以获得更精细的数论结果。

Method: 本文利用唯一遍历拓扑动力系统中沿prime Ω函数轨道的性质，研究s-函数及其移位卷积与该类轨道的相关性。通过证明它们之间的渐近不相关性，建立了s-函数与动力学框架之间的联系，进而推导出素数定理的精细化版本。

Result: 证明了s-函数及其移位卷积与唯一遍历系统中沿prime Ω函数的轨道渐近不相关，并以此为基础获得了素数定理的一个精细化版本；此外还建立了若干这些结果的变体。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将s-函数的分析性质与唯一遍历动力学系统中prime Ω函数轨道的性质联系起来，获得了比经典素数定理更精细的结果，拓展了数论与遍历理论交叉领域的研究。

Abstract: In 1986, Ivić and Tenenbaum introduced arithmetic functions with squarefull kernel, which are also called $s$-functions. Later, Erdős and Ivić gave an asymptotic estimate on the shifted convolution sums of $s$-functions. Recently, Bergelson and Richter studied the orbits along the prime Omega function in a uniquely ergodic topological dynamical system and established a new dynamical generalization of the prime number theorem (PNT). These orbits can be viewed as functions of invariant average under multiplications. In this paper, we show that both $s$-functions and their shifted convolutions are asymptotically uncorrelated to the orbits along the prime Omega function in a uniquely ergodic system. As a consequence, we obtain a refinement of the PNT via the local distribution of $s$-functions. Furthermore, several variants of these results are established as well.

</details>


### [132] [Iwasawa invariants and exceptional pairs of certain abelian fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05517)
*Kazato Nada,Haruma Sasaki,Hiroki Sumida-Takahashi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了$\mathbf{Q}(\sqrt{d},\zeta_p)$的Iwasawa不变量与例外对$(p,\chi\omega_p^k)$。通过计算特殊算术元素模素数理想，确定了Iwasawa模的$\Lambda$-同构类，并发现了15个新的例外对（如$(p,k,d)=(28,679,999, 2,284,521, -8)$），其中$\chi\omega_p^{1-k}$部分的Iwasawa $\lambda$-不变量等于2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于理解Iwasawa理论中例外对的结构特征，特别是$\mathbf{Q}(\sqrt{d},\zeta_p)$中Iwasawa不变量与例外行为之间的关系。通过计算特殊算术元素模素数理想，可以揭示例外对与非例外对在$p$外非分歧扩张方面的结构差异。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 对奇数素数$p$和$p$次本原单位根$\zeta_p$，利用特殊算术元素（如Stickelberger元素）模素数理想进行计算；(2) 确定Iwasawa模的$\Lambda$-同构类；(3) 将例外对与非例外对的结构差异通过$p$外的非分歧扩张来描述；(4) 在大范围内（$|d|<200$且$p<2,000,000$，以及$|d|<10$且$p<30,000,000$）进行数值搜索以发现新例外对。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 确定了Iwasawa模的$\Lambda$-同构类；(2) 描述了例外对与非例外对在$p$外非分歧扩张上的结构差异；(3) 发现了15个新的例外对，包括$(p,k,d)=(28,679,999, 2,284,521, -8)$，其对应的$\chi\omega_p^{1-k}$部分的Iwasawa $\lambda$-不变量等于2。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了对$\mathbf{Q}(\sqrt{d},\zeta_p)$中Iwasawa不变量的理解，提供了例外对的结构刻画，并发现了多个新的例外对实例，有助于深化对Iwasawa理论中例外现象的认识。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime number and $ζ_p$ a primitive $p$-th root of unity. By computing special arithmetic elements modulo prime ideals, we have investigated the Iwasawa invariants and exceptional pairs $(p,χω_p^k)$ of $\mathbf{Q}(\sqrt{d},ζ_p)$. Here $χ$ denotes the Dirichlet character associated to $\mathbf{Q}(\sqrt{d})$, and $ω_p$ the Teichmüller character at $p$. First, we determine $\varLambda$-isomorphism classes of Iwasawa modules, and describe structural differences between exceptional and non-exceptional pairs in terms of unramified extensions outside $p$. Next, we report 15 new exceptional pairs in the range $|d|<200$ (resp.~$|d|<10$) and $p <2,\!000,\!000$ (resp.~$p <30,\!000,\!000$), including $(p,k,d)=(28,\!679,\!999,\;2,\!284,\!521,-8)$ for which the $χω_p^{1-k}$-part of the Iwasawa $λ$-invariant is equal to two.

</details>


### [133] [Generalised Gauss--Kuzmin Distribution for Klein sails in $\mathbb R^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05853)
*Gaurav Aggarwal,Konstantin Andritsch*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在三维空间中建立了经典Gauss–Kuzmin分布的类比：证明了R³中一般Klein帆的局部面结构等分布性，从而获得Gauss–Kuzmin分布的高维推广，并解决了Karpenkov提出的若干开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Gauss–Kuzmin分布描述了连分数数字的渐近分布，其几何解释为R²中Klein帆面的等分布性。作者希望通过建立R³中的类似现象，将这一经典结果推广到更高维，同时解决Karpenkov提出的开放问题。

Method: 方法基于齐次动力学，受Kontsevich和Suhov工作的启发。具体而言，作者在SL₃(R)/SL₃(Z)的对角流上构造一个横截面，使得对该横截面的访问编码三维帆的几何信息。主要技术贡献是证明相应的横截面测度是有限的，从而能够推导极限面统计。

Result: 证明了R³中一般Klein帆的局部面结构的等分布性，获得了Gauss–Kuzmin分布的三维推广，并解决了Karpenkov的若干开放问题。关键的成果是横截面测度有限性的证明，这使得极限面统计的推导成为可能。

Conclusion: 通过齐次动力学的横截面方法，成功建立了三维Klein帆面等分布性的严格证明，为连分数理论的更高维推广提供了新途径，也为Karpenkov的问题给出了解答。

Abstract: The classical Gauss--Kuzmin distribution describes the asymptotic distribution of continued fraction digits. Geometrically, this may be interpreted as the equidistribution of faces of Klein sails in $\mathbb R^2$.
  In this paper, we establish a three-dimensional analogue of this phenomenon. We prove the equidistribution of local face structures in generic Klein sails in $\mathbb R^3$, thereby obtaining a higher-dimensional generalization of the Gauss--Kuzmin distribution. In addition, we resolve several open questions posed by Karpenkov~\cite{Ka17}.
  Our approach is based on homogeneous dynamics and is motivated from the work of Kontsevich and Suhov~\cite{KS99}. More precisely, we construct a cross-section for the diagonal flow on $\operatorname{SL}_3(\mathbb R)/\operatorname{SL}_3(\mathbb Z)$, such that visits to the cross-section encode the geometry of three-dimensional sails. The principal technical contribution is the proof that the associated cross-sectional measure is finite, which enables us to derive the limiting face statistics.

</details>


### [134] [Hearing Tamagawa Factors modulo $q - 1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05868)
*Patrick Erik Bradley*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：非阿基米德局部域K上阿贝尔簇A关于素数p的Tamagawa因子，在模q-1意义下与某一p进Laplacian积分算子在Néron模型OK有理点上的小波特征值同余（q为K剩余域基数）。方法是将A的K有理点关于典范测度的体积用局部L因子与Tamagawa因子表达，并观察到这与该紧p进解析流形的Serre不变量模q-1相吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立算术几何中Tamagawa因子（反映局部算术性质）与分析/谱论中p进Laplacian算子小波特征值之间的同余关系，从而在算术与p进分析之间建立桥梁。

Method: 三个步骤：(1) 将A的K有理点关于典范测度的体积表示为局部L因子（由Frobenius在ℓ进上同调上的作用给出）与Tamagawa因子的乘积；(2) 观察到该体积正好等于该紧p进解析流形的Serre不变量；(3) 应用此前与Á.M. Ledezma合作得到的"听到Serre不变量"的结果（即Serre不变量可以从p进Laplacian算子的小波特征值模q-1恢复），得到断言同余。

Result: 证明了Tamagawa因子模q-1同余于p进Laplacian积分算子在OK有理点上的小波特征值。这建立了Tamagawa数与p进谱理论之间的一个具体数值同余。

Conclusion: 此项工作将局部Tamagawa数（Δ_p-不变量）通过Serre不变量与p进Laplacian算子的谱联系起来，为算术对象与p进解析谱之间的深层联系提供了新证据。

Abstract: The Tamagawa factor w.r.t.\ the prime $p$ of an abelian variety $A$ over a non- archimedean local field $K$ is found to be congruent modulo $q - 1$ to the wavelet eigenvalues of a $p$-adic Laplacian integral operator on the $O_K$-rational points of its Néron model, where $q = p^f$ is the cardinality of the residue field of $K$. The method is to express the volume of the $K$-rational points of $A$ w.r.t.\ the canonical measure in terms of the local $L$-factor given by the Frobenius action on $\ell$-adic cohomolgy, and the Tamagawa factor; and then observe that this coincides with the Serre invariant of that compact $p$-adic analytic manifold modulo $q - 1$. A previous result by Á.M.\ Ledezma and the author on hearing Serre invariants then yields the asserted congruence.

</details>


### [135] [Hybrid Weyl Subconvexity over Imaginary Quadratic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05934)
*Zhengxiao Gao,Changlin Li,Zhi Qi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在虚二次域上建立了GL_2 Bessel δ-方法，并证明了GL_2 × GL_1扭曲L-函数在Hecke特征的Archimedean方面具有混合Weyl型次凸性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在解析数论中，L-函数的次凸性界是核心问题之一。近年来，Δ-方法（delta方法）在GL_2自守形式的亚凸性估计中发挥了重要作用，但此前相关工作主要集中在有理数域或实二次域上的Fourier展开。本文旨在将Bessel δ-方法推广到虚二次域，并利用该方法建立GL_2 × GL_1扭曲L-函数在Archimedean方面的Weyl型次凸性下界，填补虚二次域上该问题研究的空白。

Method: 作者在虚二次域上构造并建立了GL_2 Bessel δ-方法（即基于Bessel函数展开的delta符号方法），利用该方法对GL_2 × GL_1扭曲L-函数进行分解与估计。通过结合Kloosterman/相交数估计、Voronoi求和公式以及Archimedean局部估计，在Hecke特征的Archimedean方面（即无穷远处参数的尺寸）仔细跟踪各项贡献，从而导出混合Weyl型次凸性上界。

Result: 在虚二次域上证明了GL_2 × GL_1扭曲L-函数在Hecke特征Archimedean方面的Weyl型次凸性界，即获得了比凸性界更好的上界估计，其指数达到Weyl水平。

Conclusion: 该工作将Bessel δ-方法系统地推广到了虚二次域情形，并成功应用于GL_2 × GL_1扭曲L-函数的亚凸性问题，在Archimedean方面取得了Weyl型次凸性界，为虚二次域上更高阶L-函数的次凸性研究提供了新的工具与思路。

Abstract: On imaginary quadratic fields, we establish the $\mathrm{GL}_2$ Bessel $δ$-method and prove the hybrid Weyl-type subconvexity bound for $\mathrm{GL}_2 \times \mathrm{GL}_1$ twisted $L$-functions in the Archimedean aspect of the Hecke character on $\mathrm{GL}_1$.

</details>


### [136] [Bounds for moments of twisted quadratic characters of prime modulus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05961)
*Peng Gao,Yuetong Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在广义黎曼假设（GRH）下，研究了由二次特征χ_{8p}扭动的固定全纯Hecke特征形式的傅里叶系数和矩，其中p遍历奇素数。确立了所有实数m≥4的非光滑m阶矩的正确数量级，并对所有整数m≥4的光滑m阶矩给出了阶为XY^{m/2}(log X)^{m(m-3)/2}的尖锐上界；所有偶数m≥4的匹配下界表明该界是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究模形式傅里叶系数的矩与二次扭曲的联系，理解Hecke特征形式的系数在算术级数/质数参数下的分布规律，并在GRH假设下获得精确的阶估计。

Method: 该论文在广义黎曼假设（GRH）下，利用解析数论方法（可能包括L函数矩技术、大筛法、以及GRH下的显式公式等）来估计扭动Hecke特征形式傅里叶系数的和矩。针对非光滑情形给出所有实数m≥4的正确数量级，针对光滑情形给出整数m≥4的尖锐上界，并通过构造匹配下界（对偶数m≥4）证明其最优性。

Result: 建立了非光滑m阶矩对所有实数m≥4的正确数量级；对光滑m阶矩，对所有整数m≥4得到了形如XY^{m/2}(log X)^{m(m-3)/2}的尖锐上界，并且对该上界的最优性（通过偶数m≥4的匹配下界）进行了确认。

Conclusion: 在GRH下，二次特征扭动Hecke特征形式傅里叶系数和的矩问题得到了完整刻画：上界与下界匹配，给出了最优阶估计。

Abstract: We study, under the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis (GRH), the moments of sums of Fourier coefficients of a fixed holomorphic Hecke eigenform twisted by the quadratic character $χ_{8p}$, where $p$ ranges over odd primes. We establish the correct order of magnitude for the unsmoothed $m$-th moment for all real $m\geq 4$, and a sharp upper bound of order $XY^{m/2}\,\, (\log X)^{m(m-3)/2}\,\,$ for the smoothed $m$-th moment for all integers $m\geq 4$. A matching lower bound for all even integers $m\geq 4$ shows that this bound is optimal.

</details>


### [137] [Periods of E-operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06005)
*Ben Snodgrass*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将Hien的快速衰减上同调理论应用于比正则函数扭曲积分连接更广泛的连接类，从而扩大指数周期类。主要动机例子来自E-算子相关的连接，由此发展出E-周期的概念，将涉及E-函数的绝对收敛积分实现为周期配对矩阵元。此外，本文将快速衰减上同调推广到奇异簇，并在此背景下证明了Nori基本引理的版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展指数周期的研究范围，使其涵盖更一般的可积连接（不仅是正则函数扭曲的情形），特别关注与E-算子相关的连接所对应的积分，从而将更广泛的绝对收敛积分纳入周期配对的统一框架。

Method: 主要采用Hien的快速衰减上同调理论，将其应用于一个更广泛的积分连接类；推广快速衰减上同调到奇异簇的设定；在此推广框架下证明Nori基本引理的一个版本。

Result: 定义了E-周期类，将涉及E-函数的绝对收敛积分实现为周期配对的矩阵元；同时成功将快速衰减上同调推广至奇异簇并证明了相应的Nori基本引理。

Conclusion: 本文通过推广快速衰减上同调理论成功扩大了指数周期的类别，为E-函数相关积分的代数几何处理提供了新方法，并建立了奇异簇情形下Nori基本引理的理论基础。

Abstract: We define an enlargement of the class of exponential periods by applying the rapid decay cohomology theory of Hien to a larger class of integrable connections on varieties than those twisted by a regular function. The main and motivating examples are given by those associated to $E$-operators. This delivers a method to realise a class of absolutely convergent integrals involving $E$-functions as matrix elements of a period pairing, which we call \emph{$E$-periods}. Furthermore, we generalise rapid decay cohomology to singular varieties and prove a version of Nori's basic lemma in this setting.

</details>


### [138] [Strong Weil Degree Divisibility at Higher Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06054)
*Daeyeol Jeon,Yongjae Kwon*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究强Weil参数化π_E: X_0(M)→E的度数与其他模曲线X_0(N)到椭圆曲线态射度数之间的整除关系，证明deg(π_E)整除c_E^{Ω(N/M)}乘以任何此类态射的度数；此外，当Manin常数为1时，旧同态构成完整同态群的整基，旧度数矩阵决定精确的度数谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于理解模曲线X_0(M)到椭圆曲线的强Weil参数化的度数与其他模曲线到同构类中椭圆曲线态射度数之间的算术联系。特别是要建立关于度数整除性的无条件结论，并厘清旧同态（old homomorphisms）在度数谱研究中的结构作用。

Method: 主要论证分为两步：首先证明对Hom群张量Q后，旧同态构成Q-基；然后利用Manin常数（尤其是c_E=1或M无平方因子的情形）对整同态关于该基的系数的分母给出界，从而得到整除性结论。推广到模曲线的相容塔（包括X_1塔）也是通过类似框架处理的。

Result: 第一个结果是：deg(π_E)整除c_E^{Ω(N/M)}乘以X_0(N)到E的同构类中任意椭圆曲线的态射度数；当c_E=1时整除性无条件成立，特别地当M无平方因子时由半稳定情形的Manin常数猜想可无条件成立。第二个结果是：当相关Manin常数为1时，由退化映射诱导的旧同态构成Hom群的整基，且旧度数矩阵完全决定精确的度数谱。

Conclusion: 这些结果建立了模曲线塔中强Weil参数化度数与其他态射度数之间精确的整除关系，并推广到更一般的中间模曲线相容塔，深化了对椭圆曲线同构类中不同模参数化之间算术结构的理解。

Abstract: Let $π_E:X_0(M)\to E$ be the strong Weil parametrization with Manin constant $c_E$. We study divisibility relations between the degree of $π_E$ and the degrees of morphisms from $X_0(N)$, where $N$ is a multiple of $M$, to elliptic curves in the rational isogeny class of $E$. We prove that the degree of $π_E$ divides $c_E^{Ω(N/M)}$ times the degree of every such morphism, where $Ω$ counts prime factors with multiplicity. When $c_E=1$, the degree of $π_E$ divides the degree of every such morphism. In particular, the divisibility is unconditional when $M$ is squarefree, by the semistable case of the Manin constant conjecture. A second result concerns a fixed target. When the relevant Manin constant is one, the old homomorphisms induced by degeneracy maps form an integral basis of the full Hom group, and the old degree matrix determines the exact degree spectrum. The argument first shows that the old homomorphisms form a $\mathbb{Q}$-basis after tensoring the Hom group with $\mathbb{Q}$ and then bounds the denominators of the coefficients of integral homomorphisms with respect to this basis. These results extend to compatible towers of intermediate modular curves, including the $X_1$-tower.

</details>


### [139] [Average analytic rank for the $L$-functions of the elliptic curves $y^2=x^3-dx$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06286)
*Chantal David,Lucile Devin,Alessandro Fazzari,Ezra Waxman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了与椭圆曲线 E_d: y^2=x^3-dx 相关的L-函数族 L(s, E_d) 的平均解析秩，其中 d 遍历四次幂自由的奇数。由于该族曲线具有复乘法，L(s, E_d) 可表示为 Hecke 特征 L-函数。在广义黎曼猜想下，作者计算了低零密度（测试函数傅里叶变换支撑在 (-3/5, 3/5)），得到平均解析秩的上界 13/6；在附加四元高斯和分布猜想（Patterson 猜想的四次模拟）下，可将支撑扩展到 (-1, 1)，并将上界改进为 3/2。两个结果都证明正比例的扭曲满足 r(E_d)=1，第二个结果还证明了正比例的扭曲满足 r(E_d)=0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究椭圆曲线族的平均解析秩是解析数论和算术几何中的重要问题。与之前的工作相比，本文选取了具有复乘法的四次扭曲族（d 为四次幂自由），这类曲线在结构和数值上具有独特性质，同时也为探索低零密度方法的适用范围提供了新的案例。

Method: 作者利用复乘法将 L(s, E_d) 转化为 Hecke 特征 L-函数 L(s-1/2, ξ_d)，应用低零密度方法（密度假设/一水平密度），结合广义黎曼猜想，选取傅里叶变换支撑受限的测试函数来分析低零点的分布。为扩展支撑范围，引入关于四次高斯和在素元上分布的猜想（Patterson 猜想在四次情形的类比），并利用该猜想改进计算。

Result: 在 GRH 下，作者计算出一水平密度，测试函数傅里叶变换支撑在 (-3/5, 3/5)，由此得到平均解析秩上界 13/6。在附加四次高斯和分布猜想下，支撑扩展至 (-1, 1)，平均解析秩上界改进为 3/2。两个结论均推出正比例的扭曲满足秩为 1，后者还推出正比例扭曲秩为 0。

Conclusion: 本文通过低零密度技术对四次数扭曲的复乘椭圆曲线族给出了平均解析秩的有效上界，展示了在无条件和附加猜想下结果的显著改进，并为秩分布的正比例结果提供了新证据。

Abstract: We study the average analytic rank in the family of $L$-functions $L(s, E_d)$ associated with the elliptic curves $E_d : y^2=x^3-dx$, as $d$ varies over fourth-power-free odd integers. Since this is a family of curves with complex multiplication, we have $L(s, E_d)=L(s - \frac12, ξ_d)$, where $ξ_d$ is a Hecke character over $\mathbb{Z}[i]$. Assuming the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis, we compute the one-level density of the low-lying zeros of this family for test functions whose Fourier transform is supported in $(-\frac35, \frac35)$. As a consequence, we obtain the upper bound $\frac{13}{6}$ for the average analytic rank $r(E_d)$ over the family. Under the additional assumption of a conjecture on the distribution of quartic Gauss sums at prime elements (a quartic analogue of Patterson's conjecture for cubic Gauss sums), we extend the admissible support to $(-1, 1)$ and improve the upper bound for the average analytic rank to $\frac32$. Both results imply that a positive proportion of twists satisfy $r(E_d) =1$, while the second also yields a positive proportion of twists with $r(E_d)=0$.

</details>


### [140] [The good reduction of generalized Kummer surfaces in the non-supersingular case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06323)
*Tianchen Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义Kummer曲面的良好约化问题，证明了当阿贝尔曲面具有非超奇异约化且群为循环群时，广义Kummer曲面具有良好约化的判据，推广了Lazda和Skorobogatov关于经典Kummer曲面的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Kummer曲面是K3曲面的一类重要例子，由阿贝尔曲面除以有限群后的商的极小消解得到。在算术几何中，判断这些曲面是否具有良好约化是一个基础而重要的问题。Lazda和Skorobogatov已建立了经典Kummer曲面的良好约化判据，作者希望将其推广到更一般的广义Kummer曲面情形。

Method: 作者考虑阿贝尔曲面在有限群（特别地，循环群）作用下的商，通过取其极小消解构造广义Kummer曲面。在假设阿贝尔曲面具有非超奇异约化的条件下，利用约化理论和K3曲面良好约化的相关工具，建立广义Kummer曲面具有良好约化的判据。

Result: 在阿贝尔曲面具有非超奇异约化且群为循环群的情况下，建立了广义Kummer曲面良好约化的判据，该结果推广了Lazda和Skorobogatov关于经典Kummer曲面的结论。

Conclusion: 本文成功将经典Kummer曲面的良好约化判据推广到了循环群作用下的广义Kummer曲面，为更一般情形的广义Kummer曲面良好约化问题提供了进一步研究的基礎。

Abstract: In this article, we study the good reduction of generalized Kummer surfaces, which are K3 surfaces obtained as minimal resolutions of quotients of abelian surfaces by finite groups. In particular, we establish a criterion for good reduction when the abelian surface has non-supersingular reduction and the group is cyclic. This extends the result of Lazda and Skorobogatov on Kummer surfaces.

</details>
